# Classical Yoga Rule Book & Breaker Manual
*Authoritative Reference Guide based on Mantreśwara's Phaladeepika (Chapters 6–8), Brihat Parashara Hora Shastra, and Vic DiCara's "Yogas: The Power of Strong Roots"*

---

## 1. Foundational Epistemology: "The Power of Strong Roots"

In classical astrology, a **Yoga** (Sanskrit for *union*, *yoking*, or *relationship*) is a specific team-up between planets, zodiac signs, and house domains (*Bhavas*).

### 1.1 The Tree Metaphor (The Dispositor Principle)
A common beginner mistake is treating yogas like a rigid grocery list of promises: *"Planet X sits in House Y, so you will automatically be a wealthy king."* In *Yogas: The Power of Strong Roots*, Vic DiCara explains that **a yoga functions like a living tree**:

```
                      🌿 THE FRUIT (The Planet)
                         What quality is trying to grow?
                         (Leadership, Wealth, Scholarship)
                                    │
                                    │
                      🌲 THE BRANCH (The House / Angle)
                         Where does it sit in the world?
                         (Kendras = Worldly Stage / Prominence)
                         (Trikoṇas = Divine Grace / Dharma)
                                    │
                                    │
                      🪵 THE TRUNK & ROOTS (The Dispositor Chain)
                         Who is the landlord of the sign?
                         (Rock-solid roots = Enduring legacy)
                         (Rotten roots = The tree collapses)
```

1. **The Fruit (The Planet):** Shows what natural talent or drive is present.
2. **The Branch (The House / Angle):** 
   - Angular houses (*Kendras* — Houses 1, 4, 7, 10) provide visibility, social prominence, and real-world leverage.
   - Trine houses (*Trikoṇas* — Houses 1, 5, 9) provide divine grace, inspiration, and alignment with righteous duty (*Dharma*).
3. **The Trunk & Roots (The Dispositor Chain):** The planet that rules the zodiac sign where another planet sits is called its **Dispositor** (the "landlord" or "host").
   - **A planet is only as strong as its landlord.**
   - If the landlord is bankrupt (debilitated/fallen, deeply burnt by the Sun, or lost in houses of disease and loss: 6, 8, 12), the roots are rotten. The tree cannot bear fruit, no matter how shiny the leaves look on paper.
   - If the landlord is strong (exalted, in its own home sign, or sitting comfortably in an angle or trine), the roots are rock-solid. Even under severe stress, the yoga weathers the storm and manifests lasting authority.

---

### 1.2 The "Combination Lock" Fallacy vs. Astrological Gradients
Vic DiCara repeatedly cautions against treating classical yogas as mechanical "combination locks" or magic formulas:
> *"The yoga is not a combination lock or a magic formula. It is an identification of an underlying principle... Don't think of yogas as simply 'working' or 'not working,' but think of yogas as being perfectly or imperfectly formed."*

- Yogas describe **archetypes and patterns of energy**. 
- In nature, light and strength exist on a **continuous spectrum (gradient)**.
- Any mathematical score (such as Astra's 0% to 100% Plausibility Score) must be understood as an **algorithmic engineering heuristic** designed to rank pattern integrity, rather than an ancient dogmatic arithmetic canon.

---

## 2. What Defines a "Functioning" Yoga?

For a yoga to be truly **functioning** (active, alive, and capable of delivering its promises in a person's life), it must satisfy four core pillars:

1. **Root Vitality (Host Dispositor Support):**
   - The host planet must not be fallen (*Nīca*) without redemption, deeply combust (*Astaṅgata*), or trapped in an evil house without purpose.
2. **The Balance of Goodness vs. Prominence:**
   - **Goodness (Dignity & Beneficence):** Being in a strong sign (exaltation, own sign, or friendly sign) makes a planet produce positive, high-quality character traits.
   - **Prominence (Angularity & Shadbala):** Sitting in an angular house (*Kendra*) or possessing high kinetic muscle (*Shadbala* — the 6-fold measure of planetary potency) makes a planet loud, influential, and publicly visible.
   - *Goodness without Prominence* = A gentle, noble thinker whose ideas remain private and quiet.
   - *Prominence without Goodness* = A loud, aggressive mover whose actions generate disruption and conflict.
   - *A True Functioning Yoga unites both*: Prominent worldly action anchored in noble character.
3. **Purity from Saboteurs (*Unafflicted Condition*):**
   - The yoga must not be corrupted by intrusive aspects or conjunctions from "saboteur houses" (the *Triṣaḍāya* — Houses 3, 6, and 11), deep combustion from the Sun, or malefic compression (*Pāpa Kartarī*).
4. **Timing of Activation (*Daśā Timing*):**
   - Yogas do not run at full throttle every single day. They awaken when the clock of life reaches their planetary cycle (*Vimshottari Daśā* and *Antardaśā*). The main planet and its host dispositor act as the delivery team.

---

## 3. The Science of Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi (Continuous Aspect Gradients)

### 3.1 The Fallacy of Arbitrary "Orbs"
Many modern westernized systems use crude cutoffs called "orbs" (e.g., ±6° or ±8°), treating an aspect as a digital ON/OFF switch. Classical Vedic astrology does **not** use fixed box-orbs. 

As explained by Mantreswara and Vic DiCara, planetary aspects operate on a **continuous linear gradient** called **Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi** (exact calculated aspect power, measured in *Virūpas* from 0 to 60, where 60 Virūpas = 100% full gaze):

```
Aspect Power (Virūpas)
  60 ┼                                                  ● (180° Full Vision)
     │                                                ╱
  45 ┼──────────────────────────────────────────────● (172.5°: 75% Palpable Threshold)
     │                                            ╱
  30 ┼                                          ● (165°: 50% Weak/Marginal)
     │                                        ╱
  15 ┼                                      ╱
     │                                    ╱
   0 ┼──────────────────────────────────● (150°: Blind Spot / 0 Virūpas)
     └──────┬──────────┬──────────┬─────┴──────────┬──────────┬─────
           30°        60°        90°   150°       165°       180°
```

- **The Exact Line of Sight (180° / 7th House):** Every planet sees directly in front of itself with **100% vision (60 Virūpas)**.
- **The Blind Spot (150° / 6th House):** Every planet has a complete blind spot at 150° away (the 6th sign from itself), seeing with **0% vision (0 Virūpas)**.
- **The Gradient (Between 150° and 180°):**
  - Moving from 150° to 180°, planetary vision increases linearly by **2 Virūpas per degree** (or 3.33% per degree).
  - At 165° (halfway), the aspect is at 50% strength (30 Virūpas).
  - At 172.5°, the aspect reaches **75% strength (45 Virūpas)**.

### 3.2 The 75% Functional Aspect Threshold ($\ge 45$ Virūpas)
In Lecture 7, when asked whether weak aspects (such as 40% or 50%) can form a genuine yoga, Vic DiCara explicitly states:
> *"40% not really. You want to have a pretty palpable aspect, which generally is three-quarters or more, or 75% or more [$\ge 45$ Virūpas]."*

- **Why this matters:**
  If Planet A is at **1° Cancer** and Planet B is at **29° Capricorn**:
  - In crude sign-based astrology, people assume: *"They are in opposite signs! That's a full mutual aspect Rāja Yoga!"*
  - But their exact separation is: $29^\circ - 1^\circ = 152^\circ$!
  - Being only $2^\circ$ past the 150° blind spot, the aspect has an intensity of only **4 Virūpas (6.7% power)**! The two planets are practically invisible to each other.
- **The Classical Standard:** A yoga that depends on mutual aspect requires a **palpable connection ($\ge 45$ Virūpas / 75% power)** from both participating planets to be considered a functional, verified yoga. A score between 30 and 44 Virūpas indicates only a faint or marginal influence.

### 3.3 Special Planetary Gazes (*Viśeṣa Dṛṣṭi*)
Certain planets possess special full-power gazes at distinct angles:
- **Mars (Maṅgala):** Casts full 60-Virūpa aspects onto the **4th house (90°)** and **8th house (210°)**.
- **Jupiter (Guru):** Casts full 60-Virūpa aspects onto the trines: **5th house (120°)** and **9th house (240°)**.
- **Saturn (Śani):** Casts full 60-Virūpa aspects onto the **3rd house (60°)** and **10th house (270°)**.

---

## 4. Master Catalog of Yogas (*Phaladeepika* Chapters 6–8)

---

### Category 1: Pañca Mahāpuruṣa Yogas (Five Great Human Archetypes)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.1–4; BPHS Ch. 75.*
*Rule:* One of the five physical planets must be in an angular house (*Kendra*: 1, 4, 7, 10) from the Ascendant (*Lagna*) **or** the Moon (*Chandra*), AND occupy its own home sign (*Sva-kṣetra*) or exaltation sign (*Uccha*).

| Yoga Name | Planet | Dignity Signs | Archetype & Tangible Manifestation |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Rucaka Yoga** | Mars | Aries, Scorpio (own), Capricorn (exalted) | Bold warrior, athletic stamina, victorious over rivals, executive commander who fearlessly breaks through obstacles. |
| **Bhadra Yoga** | Mercury | Gemini, Virgo (own & exalted) | Razor-sharp analyst, master of mathematics, eloquent speaker, highly hygienic, praised by intellectuals. |
| **Haṃsa Yoga** | Jupiter | Sagittarius, Pisces (own), Cancer (exalted) | The "spiritual swan"; profound moral integrity (*Dharma*), revered by virtuous people, philanthropic guide. |
| **Mālavya Yoga** | Venus | Taurus, Libra (own), Pisces (exalted) | Refined aesthetics, charming diplomat, luxury, serene senses, fortunate in love, marriage, and financial partnerships. |
| **Śaśa Yoga** | Saturn | Capricorn, Aquarius (own), Libra (exalted) | Master of endurance, tireless work ethic, commands the respect of workers and common people, outlasts adversity. |

*Crucial Caveat:* Deep combustion (< 3° from Sun) incinerates the independent rays of the planet, ruining the Mahapurusha status.

#### Astronomical Modality Constraints (Vic DiCara, Lecture 4)
Not all rising signs can produce all five Mahāpuruṣa Yogas. Because angular houses (*Kendras*) share the same quadruplicity (modality) as the Ascendant:
- **Venus (*Mālavya Yoga*):** Can occur for **all 12 Ascendants**, because its signs of dignity span all three modalities (Taurus = Fixed, Libra = Cardinal, Pisces = Dual).
- **Mars, Jupiter, and Saturn:** Can occur for **8 Ascendants each** (their own and exaltation signs span two distinct modalities).
- **Mercury (*Bhadra Yoga*):** Can **ONLY occur for the 4 Dual / Mutable Ascendants (Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces)**.
  - *Mathematical & Geometric Proof:* Mercury's own and exaltation signs (Gemini and Virgo) are both mutable signs. For Cardinal Ascendants (Aries, Cancer, Libra, Capricorn), Kendras are strictly Cardinal. For Fixed Ascendants (Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, Aquarius), Kendras are strictly Fixed. Thus, Mercury in Gemini or Virgo can *never* fall into an angular house (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Ascendant for any Cardinal or Fixed chart!
  - *Engine Benefit:* Astra's detection engine checks this geometric constraint to instantly eliminate false positives for Ascendant-based Bhadra Yoga.

---

### Category 2: Lunar Flanking Yogas (Chandra Yogas)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.5–7.*
*Rule:* Planets surrounding the emotional mind (*Chandra* / the Moon). The Sun, Rahu, and Ketu are excluded from forming these.

1. **Sunaphā Yoga:** Non-Sun physical planets occupy the **2nd house from the Moon**.
   - *Result:* Resourceful, self-earned wealth, clear financial mind, steady progress.
2. **Anaphā Yoga:** Non-Sun physical planets occupy the **12th house from the Moon**.
   - *Result:* Generous, peaceful, modest, free from physical disease, respected character.
3. **Durudharā Yoga:** Planets occupy **both the 2nd and the 12th houses from the Moon**.
   - *Result:* Blessed with both earning power and wise expenditure; balanced emotions and reliable comforts.
4. **Kemadruma Yoga (Lunar Isolation):** The Moon stands completely alone, with **no physical planets (excluding Sun, Rahu, Ketu) in the 2nd or 12th houses from it**.
   - *Result:* Pervasive feeling of emotional isolation, subjective loneliness, or lack of an emotional safety net; financial insecurity or having to walk a solitary path. (Paradoxically, it offers supreme potential for spiritual self-reliance and ascetic non-attachment).
   - *The Complete 4-Tier Kemadruma Bhaṅga (Cancellation Hierarchy — Phaladeepika 6.7 & Vic DiCara, Lecture 1):*
     Classical tradition recognizes four distinct hierarchical layers of rescue that transform isolation into grounded self-sufficiency:
     1. **Tier 1 — Primary Lunar Kendra Rescue:** Physical planets occupy angular houses (*Kendras*: 1, 4, 7, 10) from the Moon, serving as supportive emotional pillars.
     2. **Tier 2 — Primary Ascendant Kendra Rescue:** The Moon itself occupies an angular house (*Kendra*: 1, 4, 7, 10) from the Ascendant (*Lagna*), anchoring subjective feelings directly into public, visible life.
     3. **Tier 3 — Quadruplicity / Modality Alignment (Sign-Based Angularity):** If the Ascendant and the Moon share the same quadruplicity/modality—both in Movable/Cardinal signs (Aries, Cancer, Libra, Capricorn), both in Fixed signs (Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, Aquarius), or both in Dual/Mutable signs (Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces)—the Moon is angular to the Ascendant by modality (*Chatuṣṭaya*). This structural alignment provides independent inner equilibrium and resilience even without adjacent planets.
     4. **Tier 4 — Sub-Chart / Navāṃśa Kendra Rescue:** Physical planets occupy angular houses (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Moon in the Navāṃśa (D9) divisional chart, providing subtle subconscious stability and spiritual fortitude.

---

### Category 3: Solar Flanking Yogas (Ravi Yogas — The Parallel Spectrum)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.8–10.*
*Rule:* Planets flanking the vital core (*Sūrya* / the Sun). Moon, Rahu, and Ketu are excluded. Mantreśwara defines two complete parallel sets:

| Placement | Auspicious (*Śubha*) | Inauspicious (*Pāpa*) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **2nd from Sun** | **Śubha-Veśi:** Eloquent, truthful speech, fortunate treasury, respected by authorities. | **Pāpa-Veśi:** Undeserved insult, dampened splendor, base associations, harsh speech. |
| **12th from Sun** | **Śubha-Vośi:** Charitable spirit, sharp memory, broad-minded, comfortable sleep. | **Pāpa-Vośi:** Wasteful habits, restless sleep, burdened by taxes or penalties. |
| **Both 2nd & 12th** | **Śubha-Ubhayacarī:** Well-rounded personality, symmetrical good fortune, stable leadership. | **Pāpa-Ubhayacarī:** Constant pressure on both sides, unstable social position. |

* **Budhāditya Yoga:** Sun and Mercury occupy the **same sign**.
  - *Result:* Sharp intellect, analytical clarity.
  - *Nook & Cranny:* If Mercury is within 3° of the Sun, combustion causes egoic stubbornness; optimal separation is 3° to 12°.

---

### Category 4: The Solar-Lunar Angular Baselines (Adhama, Sama, Variṣṭha)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.14–15.*
*Rule:* The foundational vitality multiplier of the entire horoscope, set by the Moon's angular house relationship from the Sun. As Vic DiCara clarifies in Lecture 9, the sequence progresses from lowest (*Adhama*) to highest (*Variṣṭha*):

1. **Adhama Yoga (Inferior / Lowest — 0.75 Toning Multiplier):**
   - *Rule:* The Moon sits in an angular house (**Kendra: 1, 4, 7, 10**) from the Sun.
   - *Rationale:* In Kendras from the Sun, the Moon is either combust/New Moon (1st), under harsh square tension (4th/10th), or in direct full-moon opposition (7th). Tones down overall chart returns; requires conscious perseverance to manifest external prosperity.
2. **Sama Yoga (Medium / Equal — 1.0 Baseline Multiplier):**
   - *Rule:* The Moon sits in a succedent house (**Panaphara: 2, 5, 8, 11**) from the Sun.
   - *Result:* Balanced life energy; fortune and vitality reflect normal, predictable, medium returns on effort.
3. **Variṣṭha Yoga (Superior / Highest — 1.25 Boost Multiplier):**
   - *Rule:* The Moon sits in a cadent house (**Apoklima: 3, 6, 9, 12**) from the Sun.
   - *Rationale:* In cadent houses, the Moon enjoys harmonious, non-combust angles from the Sun, free from harsh opposition stress. Magnifies chart results; wealth, comfort, and general success expand beyond initial expectations.

---

### Category 5: Kartarī Yogas (The Universal Hemming Principle)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.11–13.*
*Rule:* A house or planet flanked directly on both sides (in the 2nd and 12th positions from it).

- **Śubha Kartarī Yoga:** Flanked exclusively by natural benefics (Jupiter, Venus, unafflicted Mercury, waxing Moon). Acts like an impenetrable protective shield of blessings.
- **Pāpa Kartarī Yoga:** Flanked exclusively by natural malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu, Ketu, Sun). Acts like a scissor, compressing and stressing the house or planet caught in the middle.

---

### Category 6: Special Auspicious & Character Yogas
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.14–18.*

1. **Kesarī Yoga (*Phaladeepika 6.14*):**
   - *Rule:* The Moon sits in an angular house (*Kendra*: 1, 4, 7, 10) from Jupiter.
   - *Distinction:* In *Phaladeepika*, Mantreśwara names this simply **Kesarī Yoga** (lion-like). It is distinguished from later compendiums that add extra stipulations to form a separate "Gaja Kesarī".
   - *Result:* Lion-like courage, unshakeable virtue, overcomes rivals with dignity, respected reputation.
2. **Śakaṭa Yoga (The Cart — *Phaladeepika 6.14*):**
   - *Rule:* The Moon sits in the **6th, 8th, or 12th house from Jupiter**, while **NOT** occupying an angle (*Kendra*) from the Ascendant.
   - *Result:* Life feels like pulling a heavy wooden cart; fortunes alternate between sudden gains and exhausting setbacks.
   - *Cancellation:* If the Moon is in an angle (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Ascendant, Śakaṭa is canceled!
3. **Mahābhāgya Yoga (Great Good Fortune — *Phaladeepika 6.15*):**
   - *Rule:* 
     - **For a Male:** Born during the **daytime** (Sun above horizon), with Sun, Moon, and Ascendant all in **male/odd signs** (Aries, Gemini, Leo, Libra, Sagittarius, Aquarius).
     - **For a Female:** Born during the **nighttime** (Sun below horizon), with Sun, Moon, and Ascendant all in **female/even signs** (Taurus, Cancer, Virgo, Scorpio, Capricorn, Pisces).
   - *Result:* Natural alignment with universal polarities; effortless popularity, generous nature, widespread renown.
4. **Vasumatī Yoga (Immense Self-Generating Wealth — *Phaladeepika 6.16*):**
   - *Rule:* Natural benefics (Jupiter, Venus, Mercury) occupy the houses of growth and improvement (*Upachayas*: houses 3, 6, 10, 11) from the Ascendant (*Lagna*) or the Moon (*Chandra*).
   - *Authentic Three-Tiered Wealth Scale (Uttama, Madhyama, Svalpa):*
     Mantreśwara (*Phaladeepika 6.16*) establishes an authentic graded tier based on how many benefics occupy Upachayas:
     1. **Full Vasumatī (Uttama — Peerless / Immense Wealth):** All **three** natural benefics (Jupiter, Venus, Mercury) occupy Upachayas. Grants inexhaustible, self-generating riches; the native *"never lacks money, even without leaving home"* (*tiṣṭhed gṛhe*).
     2. **Middling Vasumatī (Madhyama — Substantial Wealth):** Any **two** natural benefics occupy Upachayas. Grants solid financial stability, comfortable assets, and enduring independence.
     3. **Minor Vasumatī (Svalpa / Alpa — Modest Financial Independence):** Any **one** natural benefic occupies an Upachaya. Grants modest self-sufficiency and freedom from debt.
   - *Clarification on Amalā vs. Vasumatī:* In Lecture 7, Vic DiCara's comment about Mercury being impressionable and optional pertained to *Amalā Yoga* (a single benefic in the 10th house). In Vasumatī Yoga, Mantreśwara never made Jupiter and Venus mandatory nor declared other pairings void; all three benefics count toward the graded tiers.
   - *Result:* Continuous, self-accumulating wealth (*Vasu* = real property/treasury). The native enjoys sustained material and commercial independence.
5. **Amalā Yoga (The Spotless — *Phaladeepika 6.17*):**
   - *Rule:* A natural benefic planet alone occupies the **10th house from the Ascendant or the Moon**, free from malefic corruption.
   - *Result:* Spotless reputation, revered public character, lasting legacy of civic virtue and moral righteousness.
6. **Puṣkala Yoga (Abundant Splendor — *Phaladeepika 6.18*):**
   - *Rule:* The lord of the Moon sign and the lord of the Ascendant join together in an angular house (*Kendra*) in a **very friendly sign (*adhi-mitra*)**, while the Ascendant is aspected by a strong planet.
   - *Result:* Overflowing material wealth, sweet speech, praised by kings, magnificent honors.

---

### Category 7: Ādhi Yoga (The Yoga of Command — The "OR" Principle)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.19–20, 6.42–43.*

#### The Common Misconception Refuted:
Many standard texts mistakenly claim that natural benefics must occupy the 6th, 7th, **AND** 8th houses simultaneously. In Lecture 23, Vic DiCara points out that the Sanskrit text uses the word **`vā` (meaning "OR")**:
> *"The important word there is 'vā' which means OR, not and... you do not need all three houses filled at the same time."*

Depending on which house is occupied by benefics from the Moon (*Candrādhi*) or Ascendant (*Lagnādhi*), a distinct type of command manifests:

```
                      BENEFICS IN 6TH, 7TH, OR 8TH (ĀDHI YOGA)
                      
   HOUSE FILLED       ASPECT CAST                   RESULTING ARCHETYPE
 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   6th House Alone    Aspects the 12th House        NETĀ (The Leader & Financier)
                      (Overcomes loss/poverty)      Subdues rivals, earns great wealth.
   ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   7th House Alone    Aspects the 1st House (Self)  MANTRĪ (The Counselor & Minister)
                      (Illuminates intellect)       Wise diplomat, brilliant advisor.
   ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   8th House Alone    Aspects the 2nd House         BHŪPATI (The Territorial Ruler)
                      (Protects treasury/family)    Commands land/property, long-lived.
```

*Note on Lagnādhi:* When calculating Ādhi Yoga from the Ascendant (*Lagnādhi*), the **Moon cannot participate as a benefic**, because the Moon is the other luminary and primary focal center.

---

### Category 8: The Trimūrti & Tridevī Yogas (Cosmic Deity Yogas)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.28–31.*
Mantreśwara aligns the three sacred trine pillars of the chart (*Trikoṇas*: 1st = Shiva, 5th = Brahma, 9th = Vishnu) with the great cosmic archetypes:

```
                    THE THREE COSMIC PILLARS (TRIMŪRTI)
                    
  1st House (Lagna / Self)      5th House (Buddhi / Creation)      9th House (Dharma / Grace)
        ŚIVA (Śrīkaṇṭha)              BRAHMĀ (Viriñci)                 VIṢṆU (Śrīnātha)
    [Lagna Lord + Sun + Moon]      [5th Lord + Jupiter + Saturn]    [9th Lord + Venus + Mercury]
```

1. **Śrīkaṇṭha Yoga (Śiva):**
   - *Rule:* The **1st Lord**, the **Sun**, and the **Moon** occupy Kendras or Trikoṇas in their own signs, exaltation signs, or friendly signs.
   - *Result:* Devoted to ascetic truth and universal welfare; fearless, radiant presence, unshakeable self-command.
2. **Śrīnātha Yoga (Viṣṇu):**
   - *Rule:* The **9th Lord**, **Venus**, and **Mercury** occupy Kendras or Trikoṇas in exalted, own, or friendly signs.
   - *Result:* Blessed by the lord of preservation; splendid wealth, magnetic beauty, eloquent speech, beloved by all.
3. **Viriñci Yoga (Brahmā):**
   - *Rule:* The **5th Lord**, **Jupiter**, and **Saturn** occupy Kendras or Trikoṇas in exalted, own, or friendly signs.
   - *Result:* Master of cosmic knowledge, deep Vedic insight, fertile mind, respected educator and philosopher.
4. **The Feminine Counterparts (Tridevī):**
   - **Gaurī Yoga (Śiva's consort — *Phaladeepika 6.24*):** The Moon occupies its own sign (Cancer) or exaltation sign (Taurus) situated in an angular or trine house (1, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10). *(Note: Unlike Parāśara, Mantreśwara's verse does not require an aspect from Jupiter; it is formed purely by the Moon achieving Mahāpuruṣa-like dignity in a Kendra/Koṇa).*
   - **Lakṣmī Yoga (Viṣṇu's consort — *Phaladeepika 6.24*):** The 9th Lord and Venus are fortified in Kendras or Trikoṇas in their own or exaltation signs.
   - **Sarasvatī Yoga (Brahmā's consort — *Phaladeepika 6.26–27*):** Mercury, Jupiter, and Venus occupy Kendras, Trikoṇas, or the 2nd house, with Jupiter strongly dignified in own, exaltation, or friendly sign (grants peerless scholarly and poetic genius).

---

### Category 9: Parivartana Yogas (Mutual Sign Exchanges — The Exact 66 Combinations)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.32–34.*
*Rule:* Two planets each occupy the home sign of the other. The total number of pairwise combinations across 12 houses is:
$$\binom{12}{2} = \frac{12 \times 11}{2} = \mathbf{66 \text{ total possible exchanges}}$$

Mantreśwara divides these 66 combinations into **three mutually exclusive classes**:

```
                       PARIVARTANA COMBINATORIAL BREAKDOWN
                       
  EXCHANGE CLASS          HOUSES INVOLVED                          EXACT COMBINATIONS
 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1. Dainya Yoga          Involving Dusthana Houses 6, 8, or 12    30 Exchanges
                          (11 + 10 + 9 = 30)
  ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2. Khala Yoga           Involving House 3 with Auspicious Houses 8 Exchanges
                          (House 3 with {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11})
  ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3. Mahā Yoga            Strictly among the 8 Auspicious Houses   28 Exchanges
                          Binomial(8, 2) = (8 × 7) / 2 = 28       (NOT 30!)
 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  TOTAL                   All Pairwise Exchanges Across 12 Bhavas  66 Combinations
```

1. **Mahā Yoga (Exactly 28 Varieties):**
   - Pairwise exchanges strictly among the 8 auspicious houses {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11}.
   - *Result:* Royal elevation, enduring prosperity, and divine protection.
2. **Khala Yoga (Exactly 8 Varieties):**
   - Exchanges between the **3rd house** (courage, hustle, restless drive) and any of the 8 auspicious houses.
   - *Result:* Alternating cycles of arrogance and defeat; hard work punctuated by fluctuating fortune.
3. **Dainya Yoga (Exactly 30 Varieties):**
   - Exchanges involving difficult crisis houses (**6th, 8th, or 12th**).
   - *Result:* Intermittent hardships, unexpected expenditure, chronic friction, or health vulnerability.

---

### Category 10: Dispositor Root Yogas — Kāhala & Parvata
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.35–36.*
*The Ultimate Proof of the "Strong Roots" Doctrine!*

- **Kāhala Yoga ("Large / Drum Yoga" — Dual Scriptural Traditions):**
  In *Phaladeepika 6.35*, Mantreśwara records two distinct classical traditions for Kāhala Yoga:
  - **Variant A (Mutual Kendra Alliance):** The lords of the 4th and 10th houses occupy mutual Kendras (angles) from one another, with the 1st House Lord fortified in strength.
  - **Variant B (The Dispositor Chain — "Strong Roots"):** The planet that rules the sign of the 1st House Lord (the **dispositor of the Lagna Lord**) is exalted or in its own sign, AND occupies an angular (*Kendra*: 1, 4, 7, 10) or trinal (*Trikoṇa*: 1, 5, 9) house.
  - *Astra Architecture Note:* Vic DiCara specifically focuses on and translates **Variant B** because it embodies the foundational "Strong Roots" dispositor doctrine. Both variants are scripturally authentic; Astra designates Variant B as its primary dispositor engine archetype while recognizing Variant A as the mutual Kendra alliance variant.
  - *Result:* Solid roots, unwavering courage, organizational stamina, executive stability, and ability to lead teams to prominent success.

- **Parvata Yoga ("Mountainous / Enduring Yoga"):**
  - *Rule:* BOTH the **1st House Lord** AND its **host dispositor** are in high dignity (own sign or exaltation) AND situated in an angle (*Kendra*) or trine (*Trikoṇa*)!
  - *Result:* Unshakeable nobility like a mountain; continuous prosperity, high public standing, and a legacy that outlasts generations.
  - *The "Self-Dispositor" Loophole (Vic DiCara, Lecture 1):*
    When the 1st House Lord sits in the 1st House in its own home sign (for example, Mars in Aries rising, Saturn in Capricorn rising, or Jupiter in Sagittarius rising):
    1. The 1st Lord is in its own sign in an angular house (1st).
    2. Its host dispositor is *itself*, which is also in its own sign in an angle!
    On paper, a naive algorithm blindly flags this as a full Parvata Yoga. However, Vic DiCara specifically warns against this software trap:
    > *"A kind of loophole for Parvata yoga is simply to have the first lord in the first house. But I would NOT treat it with the same significance! It is a super nice thing to have the first lord in the first house, but it's not quite as significant as when you actually get it to happen by an authentic multi-tier configuration of things."*
    - **Engine Calibration:** An authentic Parvata Yoga requires an external, two-tier dispositor chain (Lagna Lord hosted by a separate, dignified landlord in Kendra/Koṇa), demonstrating generational root strength. The 1st Lord in 1st in own sign is classified as a **Self-Dispositor Parvata Variant**, weighted at **~70% plausibility** rather than full 100% two-tier tree strength.

---

### Category 11: RĀJA YOGA vs. ŚAṄKHA YOGA
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.37–38.*

#### Mantreśwara's Strict Distinction:
Most standard textbooks generically call any union between any Kendra Lord and any Trikoṇa Lord a "Rāja Yoga." In Lecture 7, Vic DiCara highlights that Mantreśwara has a **specific, precise terminology**:

```
                       MANTREŚWARA'S ROYAL PAIRINGS
                       
   COMBINATION                      MANTREŚWARA'S NAME   ARCHETYPE
 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   9th Lord (Dharma) + 10th Lord (Karma)   RĀJA YOGA       THE KING
   (Highest Grace + Highest Action)                        Supreme executive ruler.
   ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Other Kendra + Trikoṇa Alliances        ŚAṄKHA YOGA     THE ROYAL HERALD
   (e.g., 1+4, 1+5, 4+5, 7+9, etc.)                        Announces the king's glory.
```

- **Rāja Yoga (*The King*):** Strictly and exclusively the alliance between the **9th Lord** and the **10th Lord** (*"Dharma-karma-bhavādhipatī..."*). Represents the true marriage of divine grace and worldly authority.
- **Śaṅkha Yoga (*The Royal Herald / Conch*):** The alliance between other Kendra and Trikoṇa lords. The conch shell heralds and supports the king. While highly fortunate, it functions as an auspicious supporting pillar rather than supreme royal sovereignty.

#### Rāja Yoga vs. Dhana Yoga: The Nature of Kingly Wealth (Vic DiCara, Lecture 5)
Students often ask why Rāja Yoga (formed by the 9th and 10th lords) is universally celebrated for granting vast wealth, when wealth technically belongs to the financial houses (2nd and 11th houses — Dhana Yogas):
- **Dhana Yoga (Houses 2 & 11):** Signifies *private wealth, personal savings, investments, and personal income*.
- **Rāja Yoga (Houses 9 & 10):** Signifies *sovereign command, executive authority, and high governance*.
- **The Epistemological Proof:** As Vic DiCara explains:
  > *"A king is never poor! Even the king of the poorest country in the world is not poor. Raja Yoga gives wealth because sovereign command over territory inherently commands resources (treasury, transport, attendants, infrastructure, and revenue)."*
- Dhana Yoga provides individual savings and bank balances; Rāja Yoga grants command over the collective resources and budget of an organization, institution, or kingdom.

---

### Category 12: The 7 Saṅkhyā Yogas (Planetary Distribution)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.39–41.*
*Rule:* Governed by the total number of zodiac signs occupied by the seven physical planets (Sun through Saturn).

#### The Two Operating Doctrines (Vic DiCara, Lecture 9):
1. **The Inversion Principle:** Unlike other yogas where conjunctions create alliances, in Saṅkhyā Yogas, **the fewer the conjunctions, the more glorious the yoga!** Spreading planets across 7 signs (*Vallakī*) is supreme cultural refinement; bunching all planets into 1 sign (*Golā*) is crude and congested.
2. **The "Subjective Life Filter" Principle:** Saṅkhyā yogas are not situational event-triggers; they represent the **fundamental cognitive lens through which the native filters all human experience**.

| Signs Occupied | Yoga Name | Cognitive Lens & Worldly Manifestation |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| **7 Signs** | **Vallakī (Vīṇā)** | The Lute: Highly cultured, refined, fond of fine arts, music, wisdom, and wide-ranging experiences. |
| **6 Signs** | **Dāma** | The Garland: Generous, charitable, continually helping others, wealthy, respected in public life. |
| **5 Signs** | **Pāśa** | The Snare: Skilful in practical affairs, acquires wealth through crafty work, tightly bonded to family. |
| **4 Signs** | **Kedāra** | The Fertile Field: Agricultural wealth, practical, grounded, hard-working, supportive landowner. |
| **3 Signs** | **Śūla** | The Spear: Intense, sharp-tongued, aggressive, courageous in conflict, focused like a point. |
| **2 Signs** | **Yuga** | The Yoke: Carries heavy burdens, unconventional ideas, struggles against social restrictions. |
| **1 Sign** | **Golā** | The Sphere / Clump: All planets crowded together; crude, impoverished, unhygienic, erratic fortune. |

---

### Category 13: The 2 Mālā (Strand) Yogas
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.38.*

- **Śubhamālā Yoga:** All natural benefics occupy the **5th, 6th, and 7th houses**. Forms a garland of protection, conferring royal comforts, wealth, and victory over adversaries.
- **Aśubhamālā Yoga:** Benefics are trapped in the **6th, 8th, and 12th houses**, while malefics occupy the angles. The person's talents are submerged in struggle and unappreciated.

---

### Category 14: The 12 Bhava Good & Converse Yogas (The Unified 12 Bhavas)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 6.44–69.*

#### The Core Principle:
Every house produces a **Hero Yoga** (*Śubha*) when its lord is dignified and supported by benefics in angles/trines.
When a house lord is placed in a difficult house (*Duṣṭhāna*: 6, 8, 12), it produces an **Affliction Yoga** (*Aśubha*) for houses 1–5, 7, and 9–11.

#### The Profound Paradox of Houses 6, 8, and 12:
For houses 6, 8, and 12, when their lords sit in *Duṣṭhānas*, **the two negatives neutralize each other, producing positive reversal yogas**:
- **6th House Converse = HARṢA YOGA (*Phaladeepika 6.63*):** Conquers rivals and sickness cheerfully.
- **8th House Converse = SARALA YOGA (*Phaladeepika 6.65*):** Sincere, honest, long life, victorious without disgrace.
- **12th House Converse = VIMALA YOGA (*Phaladeepika 6.69*):** Frugal, independent, immune to ruinous expenditure.

#### The 8th House Paradox (Asura vs. Sarala):
- **The "Good" 8th House Condition = ASURA YOGA (*Phaladeepika 6.51*):** When the 8th house and its lord are fortified by benefics, it does **not** create peaceful sweetness. It creates an **Asura**—a titanic, hyper-ambitious, aggressive entity. Highly capable, but ethically volatile, easily distracted by sensual pleasure, easily angered, and prone to criticize others (*kukarma*).
- **The "Converse" 8th House Condition = SARALA YOGA (*Phaladeepika 6.65*):** When the 8th lord sits in a dusthana, the destructive volatility of the 8th is neutralized, producing true nobility, health, and straightforward success (*siddhāraṃbha*).

| House | Good Yoga (*Śubha*) | Authentic Meaning | Converse Yoga (*Aśubha* / Reversal) | Authentic Meaning |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1st** | **Cāmara** | Royal Fly-Whisk: Splendor, long life | **Ava** | Obscurity, frail body, low recognition |
| **2nd** | **Dhenu** | Milch Cow: Abundant resources, sweet voice | **Niḥsva** | Poverty, harsh speech, depleted savings |
| **3rd** | **Śaurya** | The Hero: Valorous, supportive siblings | **Mṛti / Mṛta** | Timid, broken initiatives, sibling strife |
| **4th** | **Jaladhi** | Ocean / Wealthy Home: Palatial estates | **Kuhu** | Empty Home / Dark Moon: Bereft of peace |
| **5th** | **Chatra** | Royal Canopy: Brilliant scholar | **Pāmara** | Ignorant / Indiscriminate: Lacks intellect |
| **6th** | **Astra** | The Weapon: Conquers enemies and debts | **Harṣa** *(Converse Reversal)* | Cheerful triumph over rivals, healthy |
| **7th** | **Kāma** | The Lover: Beautiful, loving, loyal spouse | **Śatru** | Hostility: Bitter marital friction |
| **8th** | **Asura** | The Titanic Fighter: Fierce, volatile power | **Sarala** *(Converse Reversal)* | Sincere, long-lived, righteous success |
| **9th** | **Bhāgya** | The Fortunate: Righteous father, divine grace | **Nirbhāgya** | Stripped of Fortune: Rejects legacy |
| **10th**| **Khyāti** | The Renowned: Praised by rulers, noble work | **Duṣkṛti** | Disgraced reputation, corrupt work |
| **11th**| **Pārijāta**| Celestial Jasmine: Continuous gains | **Daridra** | Poverty of Gains: Stagnant earnings |
| **12th**| **Musala**  | Ascetic Wand: Spiritual peace, wise spend | **Vimala** *(Converse Reversal)* | Frugal, independent, immune to losses |

*Strict Breaker Rule for Harṣa, Sarala, Vimala:* If any auspicious lord of an angle or trine joins or strongly aspects these combinations, **the reversal breaks**, because the noble lord gets dragged down into crisis!

---

### Category 15: Chapter 7 Kingly Power Yogas (*Phaladeepika 7.1–13*)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika Chapter 7 (Rājayogādhyāya).*

1. **Retrogression Sovereignty (*Vakra Bala* — *Phaladeepika 7.1*):**
   - A retrograde planet possesses extraordinary kinetic potency equivalent to exaltation, empowering royal combinations.
2. **Multi-Planet Kendras (*Phaladeepika 7.2–3*):**
   - If **3 or more planets** occupy angular houses (*Kendras*) in their own signs or exaltation signs, the native becomes a celebrated leader. If **5 planets** achieve this, they rise to royal command even from humble birth.
3. **Directional Strength Sovereignty (*Digbala* — *Phaladeepika 7.4–7*):**
   - If 2 to 3 planets possess full directional strength (*Digbala*), a person born into royalty ascends the throne.
   - If **4 or 5 planets** (excluding Saturn) possess full Digbala, even a commoner attains supreme kingly command.
4. **The Five Specific "Power Yogas" of Command (*Phaladeepika 7.8–13*):**
   - **Yoga 1: Venus Rising in Aśvinī (*Phaladeepika 7.8a*):** Venus sits in the 1st House (*Lagna*) in the nakshatra Aśvinī, aspected by three or more planets. Produces an immensely charismatic leader whose magnetic charm and popularity win over opponents so completely that challengers choose cooperation over conflict.
   - **Yoga 2: First Lord with Venus in the 2nd House (*Phaladeepika 7.8b–9*):** The 1st Lord (*Lagneśa*) is placed in the 2nd House with Venus, neither planet being debilitated (*Nīca*) or in an enemy's sign (*Śatru Kṣetra*), both possessing strong dignity. Because the 2nd house nourishes and stabilizes the 1st house, this creates an economic powerhouse, a prosperous ruler, or a protector who guides people like a devoted patriarch.
   - **Yoga 3: Mars in a Fire Sign aspected by a Friend (*Phaladeepika 7.10*):** Mars (*Bhauma*) occupies any fire sign (Aries, Leo, or Sagittarius: *Aja-Hari-Cāpa*) anywhere in the horoscope (*kujē hari-cāpa-ajē mitra-dṛṣṭē bhuvaḥ patiḥ* — not restricted to the 1st house in the Sanskrit verse), receiving a palpable aspect ($\ge 45$ Virūpas) from a friendly planet (Sun, Moon, or Jupiter). Produces a bold, assertive, commanding leader (warrior, decisive executive, or martial commander) who commands through strength and courage.
   - **Yoga 4: Exchange of 9th and 10th Lords (*Phaladeepika 7.11*):** The 10th lord goes to the 9th house, and the 9th lord goes to the 10th house (*Karmeśo navamagatas...*). A mutual reception (*Parivartana*) between Dharma and Karma that produces a beloved governor and organizer of people (*Nṛpa*), renowned for moral righteousness and civic virtue.
   - **Yoga 5: The Overpowering Immovable Commander (*Phaladeepika 7.12–13*):** The Sun is in the middle of Sagittarius (~15°, *Cāpārdha-bhāgavān*) blazing brightly above the horizon (*Sahasra-kiraṇa*, having thousands of rays) conjoined with the Moon. Because the Moon is conjoined with the Sun, it is at New Moon (*Amāvāsyā*) and completely dark and invisible. As Vic DiCara highlights (Lecture 11 & 21), this total absence of Lunar light is the essential psychological engine of the yoga: it strips away emotional hesitation, shyness, softness, and doubt. Meanwhile, Saturn rises in the 1st House with great strength (*Lagne'tivīryaḥ sūryatanayo*), and Mars is exalted in Capricorn with supreme strength (*Ativīrya*). With all malefics blazing in peak power and the emotional luminary dark, this produces an immovable, feared military commander or dominant ruler whose opponents surrender from afar out of awe and sheer intimidation (*trastāva-nāmantitā*).

---

## 5. The Science of Yoga Breakers (*Yoga Bhaṅga*)

A "Yoga Breaker" (*Bhaṅga*) is an astrological factor that corrupts, hinders, or neutralizes a yoga.

```
                      THE YOGA BREAKER AUDIT MATRIX
                      
   BREAKER FACTOR          SCRIPTURAL PRINCIPLE      ASTRA COMPUTATIONAL PENALTY
 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  11th Lord Intrusion      BPHS Ch. 34 (Triṣaḍāya)   -40% (Supreme Saboteur)
  6th Lord Intrusion       BPHS Ch. 34 (Triṣaḍāya)   -25% (Litigation/Debts)
  3rd Lord Intrusion       BPHS Ch. 34 (Triṣaḍāya)   -15% (Distraction/Ego)
  Deep Combustion (< 3°)   Surya Siddhanta           -40% (Burned Rays)
  Moderate Combustion (< 8°) Surya Siddhanta         -20% (Ego Friction)
  Host Dispositor Fallen   Phaladeepika 3.11         -25% (Bankrupt Roots)
  Dusthana Trapping (6,8,12) Phaladeepika 6.57       -25% per house
  Weak Aspects (< 45 Virūpas) Phaladeepika 6.37      -30% (Hollow Link)
  Low Ṣaḍbala (< 0.85 Ratio) Bhava & Graha Balas     -15% (No Muscle)
```

> [!NOTE]
> **Engineering Heuristic Notice:**
> The percentage deductions above (-40%, -25%, etc.) represent **Astra's computational scoring heuristics** designed to quantify yoga integrity into a 0.0%–100.0% Plausibility Score. They are mathematical modeling tools, not literal verses found in classical Sanskrit texts.

### 5.1 The Triṣaḍāya Saboteurs (Houses 3, 6, and 11)
Sage Parashara specifically warns that the lords of houses 3, 6, and 11 poison royal combinations:
- **The 11th Lord is the Supreme Saboteur (-40%):** Rules *Lābha* (desire, material gain, social applause). When it contaminates a Rāja Yoga, the leader stops serving the public good and chases vanity and greed.
- **The 6th Lord (-25%):** Injects lawsuits, debts, toxic workplace jealousy, and physical disease.
- **The 3rd Lord (-15%):** Causes restless, impatient energy, petty hobbies, and unfocused ego.
- *The Lagnesha Exemption:* The ruler of the 1st House (*Lagneśa*) **never** acts as a saboteur, because it represents the native themselves.
- *Supreme Alliance Shield:* A supreme 9th + 10th lord union easily resists minor 3rd lord distraction (penalty halved to -7.5%).

---

## 6. The 6 Classical Redemption Rules (*Nīca Bhaṅga Rāja Yoga*)
*Scripture: Phaladeepika 7.24–30.*

When a planet sits in its fallen sign (*Nīca* / debilitated), Mantreśwara explains that its weakness can be redeemed and converted into a crown of resilience through six classical conditions:

1. **Exalted Co-occupant (+35%):** An exalted planet shares the exact same sign (e.g., Albert Einstein had fallen Mercury sitting with exalted Venus in Pisces).
2. **Dispositor in Kendra (+30%):** The ruler of the fallen sign sits in an angular house (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Ascendant or Moon.
3. **Exaltation Ruler in Kendra (+25%):** The planet that exalts in that sign sits in an angle from Lagna or Moon.
4. **Exalted Dispositor (+30%):** The ruler of the fallen sign is itself exalted elsewhere in the chart.
5. **Dispositor Aspect (+25%):** The ruler of the fallen sign casts a palpable aspect ($\ge 45$ Virūpas) directly onto the fallen planet.
6. **Angular Fallen Planet (+20%):** The fallen planet itself sits in an angular house (1, 4, 7, 10), forcing the person to publicly confront, learn from, and master the weakness.

# Classical Yoga Detection & Yoga Breaker (Bhaṅga) Engine

## 1. Philosophical Epistemology & Astrological Foundation

In classical Vedic astrology (*Brihat Parashara Hora Shastra* Chapters 34–42, 75; *Phaladeepika* Chapters 6–8; and Ryan Kurczak's *The Art and Science of Vedic Astrology*), a **Yoga** (Sanskrit for *union* or *yoking*) is a specific structural combination of planetary energies, zodiac signs, and house domains (*Bhavas*).

Yogas are not fatalistic, binary "on/off" switches. A textbook royal yoga (*Rāja Yoga*) or great person combination (*Pañca Mahāpuruṣa Yoga*) does not automatically guarantee worldly eminence. Its actual manifestation in a human life is governed by:
1. **The Dispositor Tree Principle ("Strong Roots"):** A planet is only as strong as its host dispositor (landlord). If the host is fallen or ruined in a dusthana without rescue, the yoga lacks ground and collapses.
2. **Structural Integrity:** Whether the yoga is formed purely by dignified rulers in auspicious houses (*Kendras* and *Trikoṇas*) with palpable aspect power (*Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi* $\ge 45$ Virūpas / 75%).
3. **Yoga Breakers (*Yoga Bhaṅga*):** Whether the combination is corrupted, hindered, or shattered by the intrusion of evil house rulers (*Triṣaḍāya* — 3rd, 6th, and 11th lords), deep planetary combustion (*Astaṅgata*), or host dispositor collapse.
4. **Redemption (*Nīca Bhaṅga*):** Whether initial debility is converted into extraordinary resilience and late-life triumph through classical cancellation anchors.

This module provides Astra with an automated, high-precision detection engine that scans a horoscope, identifies classical combinations across fifteen categories, audits potential breakers, and outputs an objective **Plausibility Score (0% to 100%)**.

> [!NOTE]
> For the comprehensive, definitive rule book detailing all yogas from *Phaladeepika* Chapters 6–8, the *Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi* continuous aspect gradient doctrine, and the complete taxonomy of yoga breakers, see [`yoga_rule_book.md`](file:///Users/hajnaljanos/PycharmProjects/astra/jyotish/yogas/yoga_rule_book.md).

---

## 2. Master Yoga Categories (*Phaladeepika* Chapters 6–8)

### 2.1 Pañca Mahāpuruṣa Yogas (Five Great Archetypes)
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *BPHS Ch. 75, Verses 1–20; Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 1–4.*
* **Prerequisites:** One of the five physical planets (Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn) must be in an **angular house (*Kendra*: 1, 4, 7, 10)** from the Ascendant or the Moon, AND occupy its **own sign (*Sva-kṣetra*)** or **exaltation sign (*Uccha*)**.
* **The 5 Archetypes:**
  1. **Rucaka Yoga (Mars):** Bold, fearless, victorious in conflict, commands authority, athletic/military prowess.
  2. **Bhadra Yoga (Mercury):** Analytical genius, scholarly eloquence, oratory excellence, mastery of trade and details.
  3. **Haṃsa Yoga (Jupiter):** Spiritual swan, moral integrity (*Dharma*), philosophical clarity, revered by the virtuous.
  4. **Mālavya Yoga (Venus):** Aesthetic refinement, luxury, diplomatic tact, fortunate in love and marriage.
  5. **Śaśa Yoga (Saturn):** Immense endurance, unshakable discipline, commanding loyalty from workers, outlasting adversity.
* **Astronomical Modality Constraint on Mahāpuruṣa Yogas (Vic DiCara, Lecture 4):**
  - **Venus (*Mālavya*):** Can occur for **all 12 Ascendants** because its signs of strength span all three modalities: Taurus (Fixed), Libra (Cardinal), Pisces (Dual).
  - **Mars, Jupiter, and Saturn:** Can occur for **8 Ascendants each** (their own and exaltation signs span two modalities).
  - **Mercury (*Bhadra Yoga*):** Can **ONLY occur for the 4 Dual / Mutable Ascendants (Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces)**.
    - *Geometric Proof:* Mercury's signs of strength are Gemini (own sign) and Virgo (own and exaltation sign), both of which are Dual/Mutable signs. For Cardinal rising signs, all Kendras are Cardinal; for Fixed rising signs, all Kendras are Fixed. Therefore, Mercury can never occupy an angular house from the Ascendant for any Cardinal or Fixed rising sign.

---

### 2.2 Rāja Yoga vs. Śaṅkha Yoga (*Phaladeepika 6.37–38*)
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 37–38; BPHS Ch. 34 & 39.*
* **Mantreśwara's Precise Distinction:**
  - **Rāja Yoga (*The King*):** Strictly and exclusively the alliance between the **9th Lord (*Dharma*)** and the **10th Lord (*Karma*)** through conjunction, mutual aspect (*Dṛṣṭi* $\ge 45$ Virūpas), or sign exchange (*Parivartana*). Represents supreme royal sovereignty.
  - **Śaṅkha Yoga (*The Royal Herald / Conch*):** The alliance between any *other* Kendra Lord (1, 4, 7) and Trikoṇa Lord (1, 5, 9). Just as the conch heralds the arrival of the monarch, Śaṅkha Yoga announces and supports status without holding supreme sovereignty.
* **Single-Planet Rāja Yogakārakas:**
  - **Cancer & Leo:** Mars (rules 5 & 10 for Cancer; rules 4 & 9 for Leo).
  - **Taurus & Libra:** Saturn (rules 9 & 10 for Taurus; rules 4 & 5 for Libra).
  - **Capricorn & Aquarius:** Venus (rules 5 & 10 for Capricorn; rules 4 & 9 for Aquarius).
* **Rāja Yoga vs. Dhana Yoga — The Nature of Kingly Wealth (Vic DiCara, Lecture 5):**
  - **Dhana Yoga (Houses 2 & 11):** Signifies *private wealth, personal savings, liquid capital, and personal financial income*.
  - **Rāja Yoga (Houses 9 & 10):** Signifies *sovereign command, executive authority, and high governance*.
  - **Why Rāja Yoga confers immense wealth:** *"A king is never poor! Even the king of the poorest country in the world is not poor. Raja Yoga gives wealth because sovereign command over territory inherently commands resources (treasury, transport, attendants, infrastructure, and revenue)."* While a Dhana Yoga builds private bank accounts, a Rāja Yoga entrusts the keys to the entire societal treasury.

---

### 2.3 Candra & Ravi Yogas (Lunar & Solar Alignments)
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 5–15.*
* **Lunar Flanking Yogas:**
  - *Sunaphā:* Non-Sun physical planets in 2nd from Moon (resourcefulness, self-earned wealth).
  - *Anaphā:* Non-Sun physical planets in 12th from Moon (peace, generosity, health).
  - *Durudharā:* Non-Sun physical planets in both 2nd and 12th from Moon (balanced wealth and expenditure).
  - *Kemadruma:* Moon completely isolated with no flanking non-Sun physical planets in 2nd or 12th (subjective emotional vulnerability).
  - *The 4-Tier Kemadruma Bhaṅga (Cancellation Hierarchy — Phaladeepika 6.7 & Lecture 1):*
    1. **Tier 1 (Primary Lunar Kendra):** Physical planets occupy angular houses (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Moon.
    2. **Tier 2 (Primary Ascendant Kendra):** The Moon itself occupies an angular house (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Ascendant (*Lagna*).
    3. **Tier 3 (Modality / Quadruplicity Alignment):** The Ascendant and Moon share the same sign modality (both Movable/Cardinal, both Fixed, or both Dual/Mutable), creating angularity by sign modality (*Chatuṣṭaya*) that grants inner self-reliance.
    4. **Tier 4 (Navāṃśa Kendra Rescue):** Physical planets occupy angles from the Moon in the Navāṃśa (D9) divisional chart, providing subtle psychological grounding.
* **Solar Flanking Parallel Spectrum:**
  - *Veśi:* Benefics in 2nd from Sun (*Śubha-Veśi*) vs. Malefics in 2nd (*Pāpa-Veśi*).
  - *Vośi:* Benefics in 12th from Sun (*Śubha-Vośi*) vs. Malefics in 12th (*Pāpa-Vośi*).
  - *Ubhayacarī:* Flanked on both sides (*Śubha* vs. *Pāpa*).
* **Budhāditya Yoga:** Sun and Mercury conjoined (intellectual brilliance), with separation $\ge 3^\circ$ to prevent incinerating combustion.
* **Solar-Lunar Angular Baselines (*Phaladeepika 6.14*):**
  - *Adhama Yoga (Inferior / Lowest — 0.75 Toning):* Moon in a Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from the Sun (angular placement brings either New Moon combustion or harsh square/opposition tensions).
  - *Sama Yoga (Medium / Equal — 1.0 Baseline):* Moon in a Panaphara (2, 5, 8, 11) from the Sun (moderate, steady vitality).
  - *Variṣṭha Yoga (Superior / Highest — 1.25 Boost):* Moon in an Apoklima (3, 6, 9, 12) from the Sun (harmonious waxing/waning distances free from harsh angular friction).

---

### 2.4 Character, Growth & Life-Pillar Yogas
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 14–18.*
* **Kesarī Yoga (6.14):** Moon in Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from Jupiter (lion-like courage, unshakeable virtue; distinguished from Gaja Kesarī).
* **Śakaṭa Yoga (6.14):** Moon in 6th, 8th, or 12th from Jupiter while not in a Kendra from Lagna (pulling a heavy cart; fluctuating fortune; canceled if Moon is angular from Lagna).
* **Mahābhāgya Yoga (6.15):** Male born in day with Sun, Moon, Lagna in male signs; Female born at night with Sun, Moon, Lagna in female signs (universal polarity synergy).
* **Vasumatī Yoga (6.16):** Natural benefics (Jupiter, Venus, Mercury) occupy houses of growth (*Upachayas*: 3, 6, 10, 11) from the Ascendant or Moon. Mantreśwara defines an authentic **three-tiered graded wealth scale** (*Uttama, Madhyama, Svalpa*):
  - *Full / Supreme (Uttama):* All three benefics in Upachayas $\rightarrow$ Peerless, vast wealth. The native *"never lacks money without leaving home"* (*tiṣṭhed gṛhe*).
  - *Middling (Madhyama):* Any two benefics in Upachayas $\rightarrow$ Substantial, durable wealth.
  - *Minor (Svalpa / Alpa):* Any one benefic in an Upachaya $\rightarrow$ Modest prosperity and financial self-reliance.
  *(Note on Lecture 7: Vic DiCara's remark about Mercury being optional applied to Amalā Yoga, not Vasumatī; in Vasumatī, any benefic contributes to the graded tier).*
* **Amalā Yoga (6.17):** Unblemished natural benefic alone in 10th from Lagna or Moon (spotless moral renown).
* **Puṣkala Yoga (6.18):** Lord of the Moon sign joins the Lagna Lord in a Kendra or in an intimate friend's sign (*adhi-mitra*), while a powerful planet aspects the Lagna (magnificent honors and royal wealth).

---

### 2.5 Ādhi Yoga (The "OR" Rule — *Phaladeepika 6.19–20, 6.42–43*)
* **Rule:** Natural benefics occupy the **6th, 7th, OR 8th** from the Moon (*Candrādhi*) or Ascendant (*Lagnādhi*). Does not require all three houses filled.
  - *6th Alone:* Aspects 12th house -> **Netā** (leader and financier who conquers loss).
  - *7th Alone:* Aspects 1st house -> **Mantrī** (brilliant counselor and diplomat).
  - *8th Alone:* Aspects 2nd house -> **Bhūpati** (territorial ruler, long-lived protector).
* *Crucial Rule:* In *Lagnādhi*, the Moon cannot act as a participating benefic.

---

### 2.6 The Trimūrti & Tridevī Yogas (Cosmic Deity Yogas)
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 28–31.*
* Paired with the three sacred trine pillars (*Trikoṇas*: 1st = Shiva, 5th = Brahma, 9th = Vishnu):
  - **Śrīkaṇṭha (Śiva):** 1st Lord, Sun, and Moon in Kendra/Kona in own/exalted/friendly signs (fearless truth, self-mastery).
  - **Viriñci (Brahmā):** 5th Lord, Jupiter, and Saturn in Kendra/Kona in good dignity (deep Vedic knowledge, fertile intellect).
  - **Śrīnātha (Viṣṇu):** 9th Lord, Venus, and Mercury in Kendra/Kona in good dignity (splendid wealth, magnetic grace).
  - Consorts: **Gaurī** (Moon in own sign or exalted in a Kendra or Trikoṇa — the Lunar counterpart to Lakṣmī without requiring Jupiter's aspect per *Phaladeepika 6.24*), **Lakṣmī** (9th lord + Venus in Kendra/Kona in high dignity), and **Sarasvatī** (Mercury, Jupiter, Venus in Kendras/Trikoṇas/2nd with strong Jupiter).

---

### 2.7 Parivartana Yogas (Mutual Sign Exchanges — The Exact 66 Combinations)
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 32–34.*
* Total pairwise combinations across 12 houses = $\binom{12}{2} = \mathbf{66 \text{ total exchanges}}$:
  1. **Mahā Yoga (Exactly 28 Varieties):** Pairwise exchanges strictly among the 8 auspicious houses {1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11}. $\binom{8}{2} = \frac{8 \times 7}{2} = \mathbf{28}$ (NOT 30!). Conveys royal elevation.
  2. **Khala Yoga (Exactly 8 Varieties):** Exchanges between the **3rd house** and any of the 8 auspicious houses (alternating arrogance and defeat; hard work with fluctuating return).
  3. **Dainya Yoga (Exactly 30 Varieties):** Exchanges involving dusthanas **6, 8, or 12** ($11 + 10 + 9 = \mathbf{30}$). Conveys karmic hardships and unexpected losses.
  - Total: $28 + 8 + 30 = \mathbf{66 \text{ combinations}}$.

---

### 2.8 Dispositor Root Yogas — Kāhala & Parvata
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 35–36.*
* **Kāhala Yoga ("Large / Drum Yoga"):** Governed by two scriptural traditions (*Phaladeepika 6.35*):
  - *Variant A (Mutual Kendra Alliance):* Lords of the 4th and 10th houses occupy mutual Kendras (angles) from each other, while the Lagna Lord is fortified in strength.
  - *Variant B (The Dispositor Chain — "Strong Roots"):* The dispositor of the 1st Lord is in its own sign or exalted in a Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) or Trikoṇa (1, 5, 9). Vic DiCara emphasizes Variant B as the foundational proof of generational root vitality.
* **Parvata Yoga ("Mountainous Yoga"):** BOTH the Lagna Lord AND its host dispositor are in high dignity (own sign or exalted) in an angle (*Kendra*) or trine (*Trikoṇa*), granting unshakeable nobility and enduring prosperity.
  - *The "Self-Dispositor" Loophole (Vic DiCara, Lecture 1):* When the 1st Lord sits in the 1st house in its own sign (e.g. Mars in Aries rising, Saturn in Capricorn rising), it acts as its own dispositor in an angle. Naive algorithms flag this as Parvata Yoga. However, authentic Parvata requires an external, two-tier dispositor chain showing generational root strength. The 1st Lord in 1st in own sign is a **Self-Dispositor Variant**, calibrated at ~70% plausibility rather than a full 100% two-tier tree.

---

### 2.9 The 7 Saṅkhyā Yogas (Planetary Distribution)
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 39–41.*
* Governed by the number of signs occupied by the 7 physical planets.
* **The Inversion Principle:** Fewer conjunctions = superior yoga! 7 signs (*Vallakī*) is supreme; 1 sign (*Golā*) is crude and congested.
* **The Life-Filter Principle:** Saṅkhyā yogas act as the permanent cognitive lens through which the native processes reality.
  - *Vallakī (7 signs):* Cultured scholar and artist.
  - *Dāma (6 signs):* Generous, wealthy public servant.
  - *Pāśa (5 signs):* Practical, crafty, bonded to family.
  - *Kedāra (4 signs):* Grounded, hard-working landowner.
  - *Śūla (3 signs):* Sharp, aggressive, courageous in conflict.
  - *Yuga (2 signs):* Carries heavy burdens, struggles against social strictures.
  - *Golā (1 sign):* Crude, crowded, unhygienic, erratic fortune.

---

### 2.10 The 2 Mālā (Strand) Yogas
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verse 38.*
* **Śubhamālā:** All benefics occupy houses 5, 6, and 7 (garland of protection, royal comforts).
* **Aśubhamālā:** Benefics trapped in 6, 8, 12 while malefics occupy angles (submerged potential).

---

### 2.11 The 12 Bhava Good & Converse Yogas
* **Sanskrit Citation:** *Phaladeepika Ch. 6, Verses 44–69.*
* Every house produces a Hero Yoga (*Śubha*) when its lord is fortified in angles/trines by benefics, and an Affliction Yoga (*Aśubha*) when its lord goes to dusthanas (6, 8, 12).
* **The Complete 12 Bhava Good & Converse Matrix:**

| House | Good Yoga (*Śubha*) | Authentic Meaning | Converse Yoga (*Aśubha* / Reversal) | Authentic Meaning |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1st** | **Cāmara** | Royal Fly-Whisk: Splendor, long life | **Ava** | Obscurity, frail body, low recognition |
| **2nd** | **Dhenu** | Milch Cow: Abundant resources, sweet voice | **Niḥsva** | Poverty, harsh speech, depleted savings |
| **3rd** | **Śaurya** | The Hero: Valorous, supportive siblings | **Mṛti / Mṛta** | Timid, broken initiatives, sibling strife |
| **4th** | **Jaladhi** | Ocean / Wealthy Home: Palatial estates | **Kuhu** | Empty Home / Dark Moon: Bereft of domestic peace |
| **5th** | **Chatra** | Royal Canopy: Brilliant scholar, wise counsel | **Pāmara** | Ignorant / Indiscriminate: Lacks discernment |
| **6th** | **Astra** | The Weapon: Conquers enemies and debts | **Harṣa** *(Converse Reversal)* | Cheerful triumph over rivals, radiant health |
| **7th** | **Kāma** | The Lover: Beautiful, loving, loyal spouse | **Śatru** | Hostility: Bitter marital friction and estrangement |
| **8th** | **Asura** | The Titanic Fighter: Fierce, volatile power | **Sarala** *(Converse Reversal)* | Sincere, long-lived, righteous success |
| **9th** | **Bhāgya** | The Fortunate: Righteous father, divine grace | **Nirbhāgya** | Stripped of Fortune: Rejects legacy, misfortune |
| **10th**| **Khyāti** | The Renowned: Praised by rulers, noble work | **Duṣkṛti** | Disgraced reputation, corrupt or wasted effort |
| **11th**| **Pārijāta**| Celestial Jasmine: Continuous gains | **Daridra** | Poverty of Gains: Stagnant earnings, debt |
| **12th**| **Musala**  | Ascetic Wand: Spiritual peace, wise spend | **Vimala** *(Converse Reversal)* | Frugal, independent, immune to ruinous losses |

* **The Dusthana Reversal Paradox:** When lords of 6, 8, or 12 sit in dusthanas, the two negatives destroy each other, producing positive reversal yogas (Harṣa, Sarala, Vimala).
* **The 8th House Paradox (Asura vs. Sarala):**
  - Fortified 8th Lord = **Asura Yoga** (Phaladeepika 6.51 — titanic, hyper-ambitious, aggressive, ethically volatile, easily angered).
  - Dusthana 8th Lord = **Sarala Yoga** (Phaladeepika 6.65 — noble, straightforward, enduring vitality).

---

### 2.12 Chapter 7 Kingly Power Yogas (*Phaladeepika 7.1–13*)
* **Retrogression Sovereignty (*Vakra Bala* — 7.1):** Retrograde planets possess extraordinary kinetic potency equivalent to exaltation.
* **Multi-Planet Kendras (7.2–3):** 3+ planets in Kendra in dignity = celebrated ruler; 5 planets = royal command from humble birth.
* **Directional Strength Sovereignty (*Digbala* — 7.4–7):** 4–5 planets (excluding Saturn) with Digbala confers supreme executive authority.
* **The Five Specific Power Yogas of Command (7.8–13):**
  1. **Venus Rising in Aśvinī (7.8a):** Venus sits in the 1st House (*Lagna*) in Aśvinī nakshatra aspected by 3+ planets. Conquers opposition through charisma, charm, and popularity.
  2. **1st Lord with Venus in the 2nd House (7.8b–9):** 1st Lord (*Lagneśa*) joins Venus in the 2nd house without debility or enmity. Creates an economic sovereign or protective patriarch whose life is grounded in sustained wealth.
  3. **Mars in a Fire Sign aspected by a Friend (7.10):** Mars (*Bhauma*) in Aries, Leo, or Sagittarius anywhere in the chart (*kujē hari-cāpa-ajē mitra-dṛṣṭē* — not restricted to 1st house) aspected by a friendly planet ($\ge 45$ Virūpas). Produces a commanding, martial, assertive leader.
  4. **9th & 10th House Exchange (7.11):** Mutual reception (*Parivartana*) between 9th and 10th lords (*Karmeśo navamagatas...*). A righteous governor and organizer of people (*Nṛpa*) celebrated for civic and ethical service.
  5. **The Overpowering Immovable Commander (7.12–13):** Sun in mid-Sagittarius blazing brightly above the horizon conjoined with the Moon. The Moon is at New Moon (*Amāvāsyā*) and completely dark/invisible, which acts as the psychological engine of the yoga by stripping away emotional hesitation, shyness, and softness. Saturn rises in the 1st House with great strength (*Lagne'tivīryaḥ*), and Mars is exalted in Capricorn (*Ativīrya*). Produces an immovable, feared military commander whose opponents surrender from afar out of sheer intimidation (*trastāva-nāmantitā*).

## 3. The Science of Yoga Breakers (*Yoga Bhaṅga*)

```
                         THE YOGA BREAKER AUDIT MATRIX
                         
      BREAKER FACTOR          SCRIPTURAL PRINCIPLE      ASTRA COMPUTATIONAL PENALTY
 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  11th Lord Intrusion          BPHS Ch. 34 (Triṣaḍāya)   -40% (Supreme Saboteur)
  6th Lord Intrusion           BPHS Ch. 34 (Triṣaḍāya)   -25% (Litigation/Debts)
  3rd Lord Intrusion           BPHS Ch. 34 (Triṣaḍāya)   -15% (Distraction/Ego)
  Deep Combustion (< 3°)       Surya Siddhanta           -40% (Burned Rays)
  Moderate Combustion (< 8°)   Surya Siddhanta           -20% (Egoic Friction)
  Host Dispositor Debilitated  Phaladeepika 3.11         -25% (Bankrupt Foundation)
  Dusthana Trapping (6, 8, 12) Phaladeepika 6.57         -25% per house
  Weak Aspect (< 45 Virūpas)   Phaladeepika 6.37         -30% (Hollow Connection)
  Low Ṣaḍbala (< 0.85 Ratio)   Bhava & Graha Balas       -15% (No Muscle)
```

> [!NOTE]
> **Astra Algorithmic Heuristic Notice:**
> The percentage deduction matrix above represents Astra's internal mathematical modeling system to rank pattern integrity (0%–100%). In classical scripture (*Phaladeepika* and *BPHS*), yogas are qualitative archetypes evaluated via gradients and root health, not mechanical arithmetic point subtractions.

### 3.1 The Triṣaḍāya Axiom (BPHS Ch. 34)
Sage Parashara explicitly identifies the lords of houses 3, 6, and 11 as the primary saboteurs of Rāja Yogas:
- **The 11th Lord is the Most Insidious:** It rules *Lābha* (desires and personal gain). When it contaminates a Rāja Yoga, the native becomes seduced by vanity, chasing superficial titles, and social posturing, abandoning the selfless duty required for real leadership.
- **Hierarchy Resistance:** A supreme 9th + 10th lord union can easily resist 3rd lord distraction, so the penalty is halved (-7.5%). A modest 4th + 5th lord union collapses under 11th lord intrusion.

---

## 4. The 6 Classical Nīca Bhaṅga Conditions (*Phaladeepika 7.24–30*)

When a planet is in its fallen sign (*Nīca*), its debility is canceled or transformed into a **Nīca Bhaṅga Rāja Yoga** if:
1. **Exalted Co-occupant (+35%):** An exalted planet shares the same sign with the fallen planet (e.g. Einstein's fallen Mercury with exalted Venus).
2. **Dispositor in Kendra (+30%):** The ruler of the debilitation sign is in a Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) from Lagna or the Moon.
3. **Exaltation Lord in Kendra (+25%):** The planet that exalts in that sign is in a Kendra from Lagna or the Moon.
4. **Exalted Dispositor (+30%):** The ruler of the debilitation sign is itself exalted.
5. **Dispositor Aspect (+25%):** The ruler of the debilitation sign directly aspects the fallen planet with palpable strength ($\ge 45$ Virūpas).
6. **Angular Placement (+20%):** The fallen planet itself occupies an angle (*Kendra*) from Lagna or the Moon.

---

## 5. Plausibility & Status Classification

Astra normalizes every detected yoga to a standard **0.0% to 100.0% Plausibility Scale**:
- **$\ge 75.0\%$:** `🌟 Pure & Eminent` (Uncorrupted manifestation during its Daśā).
- **$50.0\% - 74.9\%$:** `⚖️ Stained / Challenged` (Functional, but requires conscious effort and overcomes minor friction).
- **$30.0\% - 49.9\%$:** `🛡️ Rescued (Nīca Bhaṅga / Reversal)` (Initial hardship converted into late-life authority).
- **$< 30.0\%$:** `❌ Broken (Yoga Bhaṅga)` (Promises greatness on paper, but ruined by saboteur intrusion or collapse).
